Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any informatics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 125. The projective resolution has length at most 197 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the informatics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 125 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 197. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in informatics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 541 and the invariant I(A,B) = 414 mod 1.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
